**Read before drafting:** this movement has no source chapter of its own. Read the threads it carries: source `chapter-09.md` (the *fled* default; both roads; the first box; the carters), `chapter-14.md` (Lira's three bouts; the qualification, defined at BOOK_MAP §4a as the registry's Silver advancement evaluation [B5-reconciled 2026-10-05: audit B9] [B5-reconciled 2026-10-06: on the page it is "the advancement evaluation" or "the sitting" (B5 ch20:231, ch21:63, ch60:145), never "qualification"; audit §F.3 B9]), `chapter-16.md` (the map), `chapter-18.md` (the year's documentary work; the counsel; Havel), `chapter-19.md` (the Greyvane request; the assistant; the docket; the second Velmere envelope unopened four weeks), `chapter-20.md` (the band Ephram charted), `chapter-22.md` (Seln east twice; water at forty paces; Brom checks). Context: `BOOK_MAP.md` §§2, 4a (M7 rows), 4d, 4e (#9–11), 6 (Lira, Havel, Vastin), 9; `STATE_LEDGER.md` (after M6).

## Where we enter

The midyear recess [B5-reconciled 2026-10-05: season word struck; audit A1], the night after Ostrand. Six people know. The circle knows the corridor's doors were built before their names, and that the only clean way out opens after a ruling that will not come until the year's last month. The watchers are at the road. The faction has gone quiet.

## What this movement is for

Make the reader live inside a corridor the characters know is maintained — winning arguments they know do not matter, because leaving early would hand the machine its confession — and price each stake slowly so the walk-out is earned rather than announced. A long stretch compressed into episodes [B5-reconciled 2026-10-05: "A season" struck; audit A1]; log-voice skips are welcome.

Consequential turns (choose the route; these must happen):
- **The recess.** The circle stays on the bluff. Hesk's letter of the recess (his altitude; no history) [B5-reconciled 2026-10-05: season word struck; audit A1]. The first real argument about what knowing costs: Cael, for the first time, says out loud that he has stopped believing the text is ground — and that he will keep attending every sitting anyway.
- **Seln reads the silence**: the faction has stood down because the corridor is on schedule — "why spend a team when the seat will deliver him at the year's close." [B5-reconciled 2026-10-05: season word struck; audit A1] No third move comes; the watchers stay.
- **The merits sittings of the term after the recess** [B5-reconciled 2026-10-05: season word struck; audit A1] (two or three on the page, briefly): Jent conceding more; production orders; Karis's supplemental submissions; the counsel and Karis preparing the fossil-provision leave for the closing (BOOK_MAP §11 #4). **Havel's window** (~2k): exhibits logged; he rereads six entries; he does not write a seventh. **Vastin's window** (~1k): the proceedings' request for certification renewed; he declines in one line; nothing else.
- **Lira's return.** Off the ankle, back on the closed floor; **a return bout she loses** (~2,000) and **a bout she wins in the term after the recess** (~1,500); the registry's Silver advancement evaluation that Rooke's program is building toward now falls after the ruling, and she says so [B5-reconciled 2026-10-05: replaces the source's season-named qualification; the only qualification in canon is the regional qualifying season for the continental cycle, every three years (B5 ch1:161, ch28:193); audit B9, A1] [B5-reconciled 2026-10-06: B5's words on the page, "the advancement evaluation" or "the sitting", never "qualification"; audit §F.3 B9]; **Lira's window** (~2.5k) — the ladder, the notes she is keeping.
- **The night spar** (~1,500): Lira asks him for the rate he never shows; the closed floor, no witnesses; she decides to say nothing about it to anyone.
- **Seln's road-craft, taught openly** (new): water at forty paces; the open approach; two watches of four; **a night road exercise** (~1,000–1,500) in which Brom is the one who counts. "I check."
- **Brom**: the review's evidentiary schedule arrives; he prepares at the frames beside Ephram; **another Velmere envelope (the second to arrive this year) arrives about four weeks before the ruling and stays on the table, unopened** [B5-reconciled 2026-10-05: no series ordinal; audit B11]. No guidance note yet.
- **Ephram** charts the band in **a last wing-three bout** (~1,000) and decides, privately, what he will ask for.
- **Karis** builds the habits of a field archive without a field yet; finishes what the assistant needs; begins, near the end, to rope boxes she does not yet mention.
- **Withrow's** stack of letters from other chancellors; she answers them herself.

## What must remain true / withheld

- **Nobody leaves early. Nobody resigns. The vote is not taken** (M8 owns the council). The decision may be felt; it is not made.
- **No third off-channel action.** No new instrument served beyond the proceedings' ordinary paper.
- Brom's guidance note does not appear before M8. The second Velmere envelope is never opened.
- Anchor stays in the kit (idle state only). The band's ceiling is never exceeded in front of the school [B5-reconciled 2026-10-05: replaces "never broken"; the band is the scattered Halcenvane rate; audit A3]. Shadow sealed.
- The cache stays with Seln; nothing filed or published; nobody outside the six told.
- Havel: six entries, no seventh, unknowing. Vastin: one line of declination, nothing more.
- No [UNBOUND]; no maker; no Quieting; no Tide practitioner.
- Seln goes on filing true, empty reports.

## What the author owns

Everything else: which episodes, in what order, at what length; how much time is skipped and how; Lira's opponents; the exercises; humor and warmth across a long term [B5-reconciled 2026-10-05: season word struck; audit A1]; the weather on the bluff. Keep each episode honest about what it costs someone.

## Where we leave pressure

The year's last month opens: the closing sittings scheduled, the review docketed for the same last Fifth-day, a second envelope squared on Brom's table, three boxes roped in the records hall's north end that nobody has mentioned. Next: Ostrand, two sittings, and a ruling.
